# RS Leaders — 2026-11-16

Price data: 2026-11-16. Universe: 1460; ranked: 1378; excluded: 82. NSE common equities; price > Rs50; market cap Rs1,000 crore to Rs5 lakh crore. Benchmark: NIFTY MIDSML 400. Windows: 5 / 21 / 63 / 126 benchmark trading sessions. RS% = 100 x [(stock end/start)/(benchmark end/start) - 1]. Leader: percentile >=90 and RS% >0. Percentiles use average ties on a 0-100 scale (singleton=100); ranks use competition ties. Missing sessions/history show N/A per period; stale stocks are excluded. Rank change is previous available report rank minus current rank; baseline price date: 2026-11-06. Turnover is mean daily close x volume over 30 observed bars, in crore. No EMA or liquidity gate. Returns use local price history, not total returns; corporate-action adjustments have not been independently audited.
